class="pb-16 pt-8"><div class="grid justify-center"><figure class="mx-auto table"><div class="image-ring flex w-full max-w-full -mx-16 md:mx-0 w-screen border-x-0 !max-w-[initial] md:w-[revert-layer] class="border-stroke-default w-full border-b py-12 text-sm *:inline table-caption caption-bottom mt-auto"><p>China should take appropriate action to stop cyber attack activity originating from its territory, Andrew Little says.</p><p class="text-foreground-secondary flex-shrink-0 ml-4">RNZ / Dom Thomas</p></figcaption></figure></div></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>GCSB Minister Andrew Little said that the foreign intelligence agency has established links between Chinese state-sponsored actors known as Advanced Persistent Threat 40 (APT40) and malicious cyber activity in New Zealand.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>The GCSB had &quot;worked through a robust technical attribution process&quot; to establish its conclusions, Little said.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>He said the government is joining other countries in strongly condemning what the Chinese Ministry of State Security has been doing both in New Zealand and globally.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>&quot;Separately, the GCSB has also confirmed Chinese state-sponsored actors were responsible for the exploitation of Microsoft Exchange vulnerabilities in New Zealand in early 2021.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>&quot;We call for an end to this type of malicious activity, which undermines global stability and security, and we urge China to take appropriate action in relation to such activity emanating from its territory,&quot; Little said in a statement.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>Microsoft email servers have been targeted and Little said the GCSB has helped the affected local organisations.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>In March <a href="https://www.rnz.co.nz/news/national/437849/microsoft-email-users-in-nz-told-to-act-quickly-after-mass-hack" class="underline-brand-hover visited:text-foreground-secondary hover:visited:text-foreground-primary">New Zealanders were warned to be concerned and act swiftly</a> after a massive email hack that was blamed on China.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>Little will not be naming the victims citing national security and commercial confidentiality.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>It reinforced the importance of organisations and individuals having strong cyber security measures in place, the minister said.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>The GCSB said about 30 percent of serious malicious cyber activity in this country can be linked to various state-sponsored actors.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p><strong class="font-serif-text-medium">Read more:</strong></p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal [&amp;_li&gt;ol.list]:!mt-4 [&amp;_li&gt;ol.list]:!pb-0 [&amp;_li&gt;ul.list]:!mt-4 [&amp;_li&gt;ul.list]:!pb-0 [&amp;_li]:!mb-8 [&amp;_ol]:pb-4 [&amp;_ul]:pb-4"><ul class="list"><li class=""><span class="unknown__pt__mark__de1bb667a8c2">The Red Line series</span></li><li class=""><span class="unknown__pt__mark__66195598b0ca">NZ risks giving China &#x27;coercive power&#x27; over economy - US General</span></li><li class=""><span class="unknown__pt__mark__399177be37c0">Chinese Communist Party spies in NZ universities, lecturers suspect</span></li><li class=""><span class="unknown__pt__mark__74545066b26a">Young Uyghur living in NZ fears his family could be in XinJiang concentration camp</span></li><li class=""><span class="unknown__pt__mark__6f74122f8ef8">Merry Xmas Mr Key - your friend, President Xi</span></li></ul></div><div class="clear-left pt-16 mb-12 [&amp;_h2]:text-md [&amp;_h3]:text-md [&amp;_h4]:text-md"><h3 class="text-md-lg leading-snug font-sans-semibold">UK also blames Chinese-backed actors for Microsoft hacking</h3></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>New Zealand joined the US, UK and the EU, along with Australia and other countries, in condemning malicious cyber activity.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>US Secretary of State Antony Blinken said it posed &quot;a major threat to our economic and national security.&quot;</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>Britain said on Monday that it and its partners held Chinese state-backed groups responsible for &quot;a pervasive pattern of hacking&quot; involving attacks on Microsoft Exchange servers.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>The attacks <a href="https://www.rnz.co.nz/news/world/437814/microsoft-hack-us-warns-of-active-threat-over-email-attack" class="underline-brand-hover visited:text-foreground-secondary hover:visited:text-foreground-primary">took place earlier this year</a> and affected more than a quarter of a million servers worldwide, the British foreign ministry said.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>&quot;The cyber attack on Microsoft Exchange Server by Chinese state-backed groups was a reckless but familiar pattern of behaviour,&quot; British foreign minister Dominic Raab said in a statement.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>&quot;The Chinese government must end this systematic cyber sabotage and can expect to be held to account if it does not.&quot;</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>The foreign office said the attack was &quot;highly likely to enable large-scale espionage&quot;.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>It added Britain and its allies attributed the Chinese Ministry of State Security as being behind the hacking groups known by security experts as &quot;APT40&quot; and &quot;APT31&quot;.</p></div><div class="font-serif-text leading-normal mb-12"><p>- <strong class="font-serif-text-medium"><em class="italic">RNZ / Reuters</em></strong></p></div></div><div class="ml:col-start-2 ml:-col-end-2 md:col-start-3 md:-col-end-3"><div class="pb-8-32 light-theme"><div class="mx-auto max-w-screen-2xl"><div
